# Research inventory

Every avenue is independently scoped. “Not investigated” is used when a question is merely named or supported by contextual literature links. External papers are prior-work references, not MNI experimental outcomes.

## Minecraft integration and embodiment

Player-compatible server embodiment, lifecycle, loader/version compatibility, native interface and Survival parity.

| ID / research question | Objective and scope | Approaches / alternatives | Prior work | Feasibility, limitations and compute | Method and completion criteria | Research / outcome | Evidence, conclusion and remaining work |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

estimate CPU/GPU memory, training compute and inference budget before running a comparative experiment. | Version-pinned loader GameTests and dedicated/integrated server compatibility matrix.<br>**Research 100% when:** Complete only after the predeclared question, relevant alternatives and prior work are reviewed, planned experiments/evaluations are conducted where needed, evidence is reproducible or independently assessed, and a bounded conclusion with remaining uncertainty is recorded.<br>**Accept:** Join, track, chat, list, die, respawn and save/load without privileged state mutation.; Online learning, imitation, reinforcement, exploration, retention and safe update policy.

Reactive control, working context, planning, goal arbitration, self-initiation and temporal hierarchy.

## Memory and identity

Server and research-runtime facilities for concurrent, identity-safe autonomous agents, shared compute and scalable experiments.

## Independent research portfolio

Relevant prior work is inventoried by evidence ID and linked in avenue rows and the model/training comparison. References include MineRL, MineDojo, VPT, STEVE-1, Voyager, MineCLIP, Mindcraft, DreamerV3, hierarchical options, continual RL, curiosity, learned multi-agent communication, Minecraft construction planning, Transformers, Mamba, Switch Transformers, Decision Transformer, Diffusion Policy and D4RL. Citation does not imply code/data reuse or project validation; inspect each license and transfer limitation before adoption.
